Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The networks operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 648. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 403 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the networks space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 648 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 403. QED.
